# gfx1201 → gfx942 route mirror

## 1. Scope and route identity

This is a source-grounded mirror inventory and ordered port design, **not a hardware parity receipt**. All unprefixed `path:line` citations refer to this branch; `FP8:`/`parallel:` identify the separate read-only `/home/kaden/qcal/release-0.4.2/wt-gfx942-fp8` tree, **not files promised in this branch**. `PLAN:` identifies `/home/kaden/qcal/release-0.4.2/subq4/review/GFX942-FP8-FN-PLAN.md`. Proposed paths are explicitly proposals. Detailed verbatim inventories: [R1 dense prefill](docs/pm-cdna/route/R1-prefill-dense.md), [R2 MoE prefill](docs/pm-cdna/route/R2-prefill-moe.md), [R3 decode](docs/pm-cdna/route/R3-decode.md), [R4 FP8 KV](docs/pm-cdna/route/R4-fp8-kv.md). Inventory aliases `gemm.rs`, `gemv.rs`, `feature_flags.rs`, `dispatch.rs`, `scratch.rs`, `kernels.rs`, `norm.rs`, `attention.rs`, `tensor_ops.rs`, `moe.rs` mean `crates/rdna-compute/src/` ; bare `prefill.rs` means `crates/hipfire-arch-qwen35/src/qwen35/prefill.rs`; `qt44_qt53_prefill.rs`, `sealed_moe.rs` mean `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/`. A continuation `:N` retains its row's named source. New twin paths here normalize inventory proposals from `pm_cdna3/` to the branch's `pm_cdna/`; this is a naming proposal, not a claimed existing file.

**The pinned native-FP8 route is the dense 27B route**, documented as Qwen3.8-27B H2 symmetric MQ4V2 XT/qt44 with per-group AWQ alpha. WT2/code24 pins are 0.041859/0.029867 under `HIPFIRE_IU4_PREFILL=0 --kv-mode fp8 --kv-v q8` (`AGENTS.md:600-601,637-642`). IU4-off admits FP8 activation producers and prepared sidecars; N≥512 selects PM **F2 Row FP8 WMMA**, while smaller eligible rows or ISA-off select hipcc v2/s2bt8/bt tiles (`crates/hipfire-arch-qwen35/src/qwen35/prefill.rs:629-641`; `dispatch.rs:4519-4529`; `gemm.rs:10263-10283,10837-10936`). This is not the IU4 MMQ path and not every projection of every model.

**`--kv-v q8` does not mean Q8 V in native mode.** Both native K and V use FP8 writers; legacy neutral `v_mode_bits==8` is a naming sentinel (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/kv_tier.rs:344-359`; `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/attention.rs:557-576`; `kernels/src/attention_q8_0_fa2_gqa.gfx1201.hip:57-60`). The eval harness requires that legacy spelling (`crates/hipfire-runtime/examples/eval_hipfire.rs:453-462`), but the typed pair resolver refuses authored native FP8 plus an authored V-axis override (`crates/hipfire-runtime/src/kv_mode.rs:272-302`). Thus the exact pinned CLI launches no decode kernels through that frontend in this snapshot; tables describe its admitted neutral-V equivalent, not a fabricated mixed FP8-K/Q8-V format.

**Flash-Next has no gfx1201 FP8 trunk arm and no FP8-activation MoE arm.** Its trunk dispatch stays BF16/MQ4/MQ6/Q8; the MoE choices are symmetric IU4, F16 WMMA gate/up, and F32 SIMT. Symmetric MoE's independent `HIPFIRE_QWEN4_MOE_SYM_IU4` can still select IU4 with global IU4 prefill disabled (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/layer_ops.rs:615-669`; `qt44_qt53_prefill.rs:1189-1245,1432-1506`; `gemm.rs:46925-46935,47165-47189`; `kernels/src/gemm_mq4g256v2_moe_grouped_wmma.gfx12.hip:141-153`). The new qt55 FP8×FP8 MoE is a generator arm, distinct from the parallel weight-only FP8→BF16/BF16-MFMA baseline (`FP8:kernels/src/gemm_fp8b128_moe_grouped_bf16mfma.gfx942.hip:17-43,86-110`; PLAN `:315-317`). No 27B pin establishes Flash-Next accuracy.

**Decode M=1/few rows has no ordinary FP8 activation pack.** Qwen35 keeps MQ4/F32 projections and Qwen4 MQ4 experts/BF16 boundaries; FP8 ordinarily denotes KV storage/readback, not activation slabs (`gemm.rs:835-839,10263-10283`; `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/layer_ops.rs:615-669`). Qwen4 gfx1201 AR lanes are all-per-lane, not automatically an M=C few-row GEMV (`crates/hipfire-arch-qwen4/src/gpu_forward_lanes.rs:211-265`). QSA FP8 caches both K and V, per-head/token F16 scales with BF16 raw/pooled index keys; D256/even Hkv admitted, compute gfx1200/1201, runtime auto exact gfx1201. Current gfx942 auto is F32-backed `bf16`, explicit FP8/Q8 refused (`tensor_ops.rs:2530-2593,2727-2747`; `crates/hipfire-runtime/src/kv_mode.rs:548-575`).

### Inventory discrepancies versus brief/plan

* **Scale bump is one `if`, not a loop:** QSA and dense writers increment F16 scale once with cap, contrary to PLAN §13.1's "until" (`kernels/src/tensor_ops.hip:3122-3151`; `kernels/src/kv_cache_write_q8_0.hip:104-157`; PLAN `:308`).
* **KV naming/frontends:** native `q8` V sentinel is FP8 V; typed-loader rejection and harness legacy admission differ, as above. `qresident_v2_q8` means quantized **Q**, not Q8 V (`kernels/src/attention_q8_0_fa2_gqa.gfx1201.hip:3159-3162`).
* **Geometry/model names:** dense 27B native admission is exact H24/Hkv4/D256 (dim5120/L64/GDN16:48); the fixture named "3.8" is H48/Hkv8/D128 and not admitted (`crates/hipfire-arch-qwen35/src/carrier.rs:780-810`; `crates/hipfire-arch-qwen35/src/qwen35/config.rs:1028-1039,1353-1367`). Flash-Next QSA Hkv2/D256 is not dense Hkv4; MoE fixed E512/top10/hidden2560/intermediate640 is not a 27B geometry (`crates/hipfire-arch-qwen4/src/config.rs:1025-1075`; `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/moe.rs:103-115,137-170`; PLAN `:308`). gfx1201 `has_wmma` does **not** mean `has_wmma_w32`; gfx11-only BF16-output/down arms are not gfx1201 launches (`crates/rdna-compute/src/arch_caps.rs:140-147`; `qt44_qt53_prefill.rs:1066-1076,1167-1169`).
* **GDN fast kernels are not all portable:** source registration enables DPP/permlanex16 in `gated_delta_net_q8_fast`/compact3; gfx942 needs legal logical32 cross-lane twins, not direct source reuse (`crates/rdna-compute/src/kernels.rs:8632-8635,8728-8731`; `kernels/src/gated_delta_net_q8_fast.hip:142`). GDN chunk scan remains a port dependency, not a gate-only widening.
* **gfx942 IU4 resolution:** `HIPFIRE_IU4_PREFILL` already resolves false (no int4 MFMA); route selection there comes from same-named family flags, with proposed opt-in gfx942 defaults, not from inventing an int4 MFMA arm (`feature_flags.rs:774,2281-2295`; R1 lever table). Brief citations `kernels.rs:4706-4818`, `scratch.rs:2198-2206`, `gemm.rs:21092-21101` are IU4; `kernel_registry.rs:392-399` is in the gfx1100/1151 block at `:377`; none identifies this gfx1201 native-FP8 chain (R1 route identity).
* **Port status distinction:** gfx1201 PM `.hxaco` is never a gfx942 twin; stable scatter algorithms still have source arch guards; generic CDNA atomic MoE down is not the fixed-order qt55 design (`crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/qwen4_moe_sym.rs:61-65,137`; `kernels/src/qwen4_moe_scatter_stable_top10.hip:25-26`; `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/moe.rs:1740`; R2 summary).


Additional corrections: GQA admission is D256, positive Hkv, **Hq=6*Hkv**, tile128—not exclusively H24/Hkv4 (`crates/rdna-compute/src/attention.rs:367-379`). QSA quantized state uses **BF16** raw/pooled index keys, not the plan’s unchanged-F32 index approximation (`tensor_ops.rs:2563-2593`; R4 admission section). Ordinary MQ4 decode rounds gate/up/final product, **not intermediate SiLU**, unlike new qt55’s declared three boundaries (`kernels/src/mq_rotate_x_128_v2.hip:70-82`; `kernels/src/moe_gate_up_unscatter_silu_top10.hip:118-127`; R3 MoE.5 correction).

## 2. Consolidated lever table

Same parser/name and semantics are retained. Route-specific defaults and resolver proposals are labeled; combined rows deduplicate aliases without erasing model distinctions. gfx942 IU4 is already false (R1); R3’s proposed IU4 architecture widening is unnecessary. New generator controls are proposals, not parsed flags.

## 3. Consolidated kernel tables by route family

Launch order includes mutually exclusive alternatives and repeated layer units, not one unconditional sequence. “portable as-is” describes HIP source/math only; **every PM gfx942 code object is new**. PM/HIP proposed designs are not runtime registration or hardware evidence. Numeric blockers are explicit, not fallbacks. §5 supersedes the inventory proposal where actual first-kernel design differs.

### Design keys and sidecar contracts


#### R1 scratch / sidecars (R1:51-60)

| buffer | layout | where | gfx942 |
|---|---|---|---|
| X8 | `[N,K]` u8 E4M3 codes | `scratch.rs:1123-1140`; grown `scratch.rs:2502-2546` | Byte-identical under the ×0.5 activation fold (D1). Exception: −0. |
| half_sums | `[N,K/256,2]` f32: sum of **decoded** codes per 128-half | same | Used only by non-symfold v2/bt. Keep in the OCP value domain (D1c). |
| row_scales (`D`) | `[N]` f32, 2^clamp(ceil(log2(amax/224)),−31,9) | `mq4v2_fp8_producer_pack.hip:70-79` | Identical. |
| F2 workspace Wf/Rw/Ew | Wf `Mp·K` B, Rw `Mp·K/128` f32, Ew `Mp` f32, Mp = ceil(Mt/256)·256 | `scratch.rs:1142-1149,1151-1161`, field `:407-409`; carved `gemm.rs:10382-10428` | Identical bytes (D2). |
| GDN fused targets q/k/v FP16, conv ring | `prefill.rs:7472-7483,7530-7543` | — | Blocked with K7c. |
| capture | Every FP8 entry is eager-only (`gemm.rs:10388-10392`, `dispatch.rs:4072-4077`, `gemv.rs:3993-3998`) | — | Same rule. |

#### R1 D1–D3 (R1:64-102)

The following design contracts are preserved from the inventory, not certified implementations. D1's exact-byte/product statements are conditional on admitted finite input range, canonical-zero handling, and the specified summation/rounding order; R2:137's exponent-clamp caveat applies to the shared recipe.

**D1 — fold placement:** see §4; D1a=producer, D1b=operand/decode, D1c=OCP half sums, D1d=scaled consumer compensation.

**D2 — F2 Row on gfx942: MFMA 16x16x32 fp8 tile mapping.**
- gfx1201 F2:
  - grid `[ceil(Mt/256), ceil(N/128), 1]`, block 256 (8×wave32), LDS 19,456 B (`gemm.rs:10485-10487`, `spec.rs:16-17`).
  - Per wave: 64 rows (rg) × 64 tokens (token half) (`gdn_epilogue.rs:20-24`). acc index `8·(i·4+tt)+e` (`fold.rs:41`).
- gfx942 twin:
  - Block 256 = **4×wave64**, same grid and LDS budget. Wave w owns rg=w (64 rows) × all 128 tokens: 4 row16 (i) × 8 token16 (tt) tiles × 4 acc = **128 acc VGPRs in arch v0–v127**. They stay out of AGPRs so the VALU ratio fold can touch them; MFMA C/D may be VGPR or AccVGPR per ACC_CD (CDNA3 ISA §7.1 p.41).
  - **A = weights.** Lane l reads Wf (bytes identical to gfx1201) at block offset `((l&15) + 16·((l>>4)&1))·16 + (l>>5)·8`. This follows from the repack layout (`mq4v2_fp8_fragment_repack.gfx12.hip:26-30`) and the MFMA A rule lane = row + 16·(k/8), K_L=8 (CDNA3 ISA §7.1.4.1 p.47). The offset is a per-lane constant, and each 512 B block stays fully coalesced.
  - **B = activations.** X8 staged in LDS: slot s (K64) × K32 step st × tt → 512 B block at `s·8192 + st·4096 + tt·512`, lane l at `+l·8` = `X8[tok tt·16+(l&15)][k0+st·32+8·(l>>4)..+7]`. That is 2×8192 B = the gfx1201 activation slot budget (`publish.rs` per PmBuilders scout: `slot·8192 + step·2048 + tt·256`).
  - **Ratios** stay at `16384 + plane·1024` (`fold.rs:28`). Lane reads one `ds_read_b128` per i at row `w·64 + i·16 + 4·(l>>4)` (the four rows it owns).
  - **Output.** Lane holds `D[4·(l>>4)+r][l&15]` (CDNA3 ISA §7.1.4.2 p.48: H=4, M_I=4) = 4 consecutive weight rows × 1 token. That is exactly the "row quad" `store_quad` already assumes (`epilogue.rs:36-38`). Ew: 16 regs/lane (4 i × 4 r). D: 8 regs/lane (tt).
  - **Per K128 per wave:** 4 u × 4 i × 8 tt = 128 `v_mfma_f32_16x16x32_fp8_fp8` (16 cycles each, CDNA3 ISA §7.1.2 p.42). Ratio fold per half = 64 `v_pk_mul_f32` (acc pair × ratio pair; VOP3P op 49, CDNA3 ISA §12.10 p.263).
  - **SiLU pairing.** The family-1 repack interleaves gate/up row16 blocks i=2p (gate) and 2p+1 (up) for the same hrow (`mq4v2_fp8_fragment_repack.gfx12.hip:18-21,60-67`). gfx1201 pairs acc g with g+32 (`epilogue.rs:151-157`). On gfx942 the same lane holds both (i=2p, 2p+1), so h = silu(g)·u in-lane with no cross-lane traffic.
  - **Register budget** `[UNVERIFIED occupancy]`: ~128 acc + 16 A (double-buffered; may live in AGPRs, MFMA src A/B may be AccVGPR per ACC bits, CDNA3 ISA §7.1 p.41) + 16 B + 16 ratio + ~12 addr. Under 256 arch; allocation granule 8 (CDNA3 ISA §3.6.4 p.12).
  - **Alternatives:** 8×wave64 (block 512, 64 acc/lane, 64 rows × 64 tokens per wave, matching gfx1201 wave ownership 1:1); or `v_mfma_f32_32x32x16_fp8_fp8` (same cycles per FLOP, 16 distinct ratio rows per lane per tile; worse fold).

**D3 — hipcc producers on wave64.**
- Every FP8 producer runs 256 threads with logical `lane=tid&31, wave=tid>>5`:
  - `fused_rmsnorm_mq_rotate.hip:149-152`
  - `fused_silu_mul_mq_rotate_fp8.gfx12.hip:48-50`
  - `gated_norm_mq_rotate_fp8.gfx12.hip:50-52`
  - `mq_rotate_x_fp8.gfx12.hip:40-42`
  - `pack_f16_to_fp8_mq4v2.gfx12.hip:211-213`
- Every cross-lane op stays inside a 32-lane group:
  - FWHT `ds_swizzle` bitmask mode (`0x041F..0x401F`) acts "on each group of 32 within a wavefront" (CDNA3 ISA §12.12 p.426).
  - `__shfl_xor` offsets ≤ 16 (`mq4v2_fp8_producer_pack.hip:56-68,218-222`).
  - The RMS `__shfl_down` ≤ 16 chain is consumed only at lanes 0/32 (`fused_rmsnorm_mq_rotate.hip:313-318`), so it depends only on its own 32 lanes.
- So on gfx942 (2 logical warps per hw wave) the arithmetic is bit-identical by reading. Tier C still decides `[UNVERIFIED]`.
- The FWHT butterfly result is independent of lane mapping. A native wave64 FWHT already exists (`kernels/src/mq_rotate_x.gfx942.hip:7-12`) if the 32-lane form under-occupies.
- The only FP8 deltas are D1a/D1d. There are no gfx1201 PM producers (all hipcc: `kernels.rs:1317-1427`), so gfx942 needs HIP twins only.

#### R2 sealed lowering / geometry (R2:51-57)

- Policy capability fixes 512 experts, top10, hidden2560, intermediate640; routed gate/up must be QT44/MQ4G256V2 and down QT53/MQ4G128V2. Symmetry means every actual header passed `zp == -8*sc`, never inferred from dtype (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/moe.rs:103-115,137-170`). Architecture binding declares the symmetric policy only at rows≥512 (`crates/hipfire-arch-qwen4/src/gpu_forward.rs:537-541,786-791`). Sealed router consumes that capability and rounds logits/weights internally (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/sealed_moe.rs:2095-2137`); specialized scratch validates before launch (`:2008-2012`; `qt44_qt53_prefill.rs:247-279`).
- `MOE_GROUPED_BLOCK_M=16` (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/moe.rs:30-31`). Flat slot is `token*10+rank`; gate/up gathers `flat/10`, down gathers `flat`. Each expert run pads independently to16; sorted padding and unused tile IDs are−1; `inverse_perm[flat]` restores actual row irrespective of scatter order (`kernels/src/qwen4_moe_scatter_stable_top10.hip:4-20,80-108`; `kernels/src/gemm_mq4g256v2_moe_grouped_wmma.gfx12.hip:47-87`). No host readback of live tile count is needed (`kernels/src/qwen4_moe_scatter_fused_k8.hip:55-58`).
- `tables/moe_table.rs` is **not the prefill dispatcher**: `BatchGt(1)` and WMMA grouped entries are documentation-only; dtype/env internal resolution is authoritative (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/tables/moe_table.rs:12-20,40-59`). Therefore adding an MFMA row only to this table does not implement a gfx942 sealed route.
- gfx1201 has `has_wmma=true` but `has_wmma_w32=false` (`crates/rdna-compute/src/arch_caps.rs:140-147`). Thus dispatch `gateup_bf16`, `gateup_silu`, and `down_wmma` do not take the gfx11 BF16-output/F16-down arms on gfx1201 (`qt44_qt53_prefill.rs:1066-1076,1167-1169`). Non-sym gfx1201 uses F32 grouped gate/up output, fused unscatter+rotation, and SIMT down. Do not list gfx11 `_bf16out`, `_silu_bf16out`, `_xf16`, or unscatter-BF16in as gfx1201 launches.
- For ≤8 rows path2 may use indexed down after grouped gate/up/unscatter (`qt44_qt53_prefill.rs:1396-1398,1465-1466,1513-1522,1574-1575`). `HIPFIRE_MOE_GROUPED_GEMM=0` also takes indexed gate/up/down. Proposed qt55 route refuses these distinct-order fallbacks and the CDNA atomic path0; one grouped kernel handles padded M1 as well as prefill.

#### R2 T55 operands, wave/tile, LDS, registers, waits (R2:98-125)

#### Operands and numerical order

1. **Weights:** qt55 row-major codes `[E,R,K]`, then verbatim BF16 `s_inv[ceil(R/128),ceil(K/128)]`, expert stride round-up256. Gate/up R1280,K2560; gate rows0..639 and up640..1279. Down R2560,K640. Scale rows align because640%128=0 (`FP8:docs/quant-formats/fp8-e4m3.md:26-50`). Natural basis, no FWHT (`:15`). Read `s_w = f32(s_inv[(output_col/128)*(K/128)+kb])`; it is a **multiplier**, despite the name `s_inv`.
2. Preserve artifact bytes. Remap weight **exact byte0x80 to0x00 in operand registers** before FP8 MFMA, retaining signs of every nonzero byte. Reject OCP NaNs0x7f/0xff at artifact admission, not by pretending their FNUZ interpretation is valid (`FP8:docs/quant-formats/fp8-e4m3.md:100-110`). FNUZ has bias8,max240,NaN0x80,no−0 (`CDNA3 ISA §7.2 p.50`). Every admitted OCP weight code reads as `fn(q)/2` after zero remap (`FP8:docs/quant-formats/fp8-e4m3.md:86-98`). **This remap is value-exact, not signed-zero/BF16-bit identity**: the existing weight-only decoder explicitly restores−0 for teacher identity (`FP8:...bf16mfma.gfx942.hip:20-27,86-98`). Do not edit stored qt55 bytes or apply the remap to the teacher decoder.
3. Activations: recipe-boundary input in natural coordinates. Per whole row `a=2^clamp(ceil(log2(amax/224)),-31,9)`, zero row→1, as source (`kernels/src/mq4v2_fp8_producer_pack.hip:70-78`). Pack RNE with `v_cvt_pk_fp8_f32((x/a)*0.5)`, canonical zero, little-endian bytes. When `abs(x/a)≤224`, finite nonzero codes match the OCP producer's code grid because argument≤112; each hardware activation decodes to `fn(code)/2` (`PLAN:315-316`; `CDNA3 ISA §7.2 p.50-51`). No stochastic conversion.
4. For every output keep two F32 vectors: `total=+0`, and `partial=+0` reset at **each128-K block**. For kb=0..K/128−1, issue four MFMA K32 instructions at k=`128*kb+32*t`, t=0,1,2,3. Then for each output use a pinned DAG `scale = ((4.0f*a_row)*s_w)` and `total=fma(partial,scale,total)`; never carry the unscaled accumulator into the next block. Scale location is perblock, **not one final epilogue multiply**, because `s_w` changes withkb (`PLAN:316`). No split-K, no atomics, no row-count-specific K order. Fourfold decode compensation is exact as a power-of-two fold; total rounding/output equality to WMMA is **not** promised.
5. Gate/up stores F32 `[P,1280]`; unscatter combines gate and up only after both complete. For the existing MQ4 boundary, g/u and final product are BF16 rounded but SiLU itself is not (`kernels/src/moe_gate_up_unscatter_silu_top10.hip:49-55`). For the stock-FP8 teacher-compatible activation recipe, use the parallel natural-basis twin's extra `BF16(silu(g))` before `BF16(s*u)` (`FP8:kernels/src/fp8b128_moe_unscatter_silu.gfx942.hip:12-21,62-65`). The native FP8 GEMMs are generator-only whichever recipe is chosen; keep one explicit recipe, never let PM/HIP select different boundaries. Pack down rows afresh atK640. Down stores F32 `[P,2560]`; existing F32-source combine BF16-rounds per-expert output, weight, product and each expert-ID-ordered sum (`kernels/src/moe_down_combine_grouped_top10.hip:43-75`).
6. This differs from the BF16 teacher even beyond activation quantization: native FP8 multiplies raw FP8 weights then block-scale-adds, whereas weight-only baseline rounds each `fn(q)*s_w` to BF16 before BF16 MFMA (`FP8:docs/quant-formats/fp8-e4m3.md:67-79`; `FP8:...bf16mfma.gfx942.hip:20-32`). Keep independent accuracy/identity evidence; no claim that the ×4 fold alone makes teacher logits identical.

#### Wave/tile mapping (same PM and HIP)

Proposed conservative geometry: block256 = fourwave64; grid `(R/64, P/16,1)`, one16-token group per expert tile,64output columns/CTA. Full gate/up is **20CTAs ×64=1280** outputs per16-row M tile; down is **40CTAs ×64=2560**. These counts are derived from qt55 shapes, not new model geometry. Under padded descriptor `{e,row0,16,0}`, skip e<0 before any load/store; zero operands and write+0 for sorted rows−1.

Use transposed hardware multiplication exactly like the existing grouped BF16 twin: hardware A=weights[16output,32K], B=activations[32K,16slot], D=[16output,16slot]; logical output is transposed on store (`FP8:...bf16mfma.gfx942.hip:34-50`). For wavew, lane l, `n=l%16`, `s=l/16`, output_base=`64*blockIdx.x+16*w`:

- A pair bytesi=0..7: `W[e, output_base+n, 128*kb+32*t+8*s+i]`, zero-remapped.
- B pair bytesi: `Xq[xrow(n),128*kb+32*t+8*s+i]`; `xrow(n)=sorted[row0+n]/10` for gate/up, `sorted[row0+n]` for down; negative slot means zero. Both pairs are64-bit packed little-endian.
- Four result registers r=0..3 represent `D[4*s+r,n]`: store `Y[(row0+n)*R+output_base+4*s+r]`. All four apply the **same activation scale of token/slot n**, and weight scale of output_base's128-output block. A16-output wave and64-output CTA never straddle a128-output scale row.
- Mapping follows `CDNA3 ISA §7.1.4 p.46-48`; actual opcode is `v_mfma_f32_16x16x32_fp8_fp8` (`CDNA3 ISA §7.1 p.42`, §7.1.4 p.46-48). Reuse one entry/shape for M1, M2, M16 and large prefill by zero-padding to16, not a separate GEMV order.

#### LDS, registers, waits

- Proposed LDS `unsigned xl[2][16][36]`: two128-K code buffers; each logical row128B+16B padding; **4608B**, plus16F32 row scales64B =4672B. No weight LDS: each wave owns its16weight rows and loads8bytes/lane/K32 directly, with next-block weights/scales prefetched into VGPRs. Codes don't need BF16 expansion. This adapts the current BF16 twin's padded row staging (`FP8:...bf16mfma.gfx942.hip:124-145`), not its exact68-dword BF16 row width.
- Within eachK128, threadtid stages row=`tid/16`, kbyte=`8*(tid%16)` (8bytes/thread). B pair read starts at LDS dword `[buffer][n][8*t+2*s]`. Pad16B changes the128B row stride; **not claimed conflict-free**. Bank occupancy/performance needs on-card measurement. Bounds/padding are fixed and independent of prompt row count. At least twoF32x4 accumulator vectors/wave (partial,total) and operand/prefetch pairs; budget final register allocation against CDNA's unified512 VGPR/AGPR pool, not RDNA wave32 assumptions (`CDNA3 ISA §3.6.4 p.12-13`; existing architecture metadata must use64-bit EXEC/VCC).
- PM: wait VMEM before consuming fetched weight/activation registers; wait LDS stores before a cross-wave barrier/read, wait LDS reads before MFMA; barrier before buffer reuse. Use gfx9 `s_waitcnt`: VMEM loads **and stores** count/return in issue order, SMEM may return out-of-order, LDS in order; barrier is workgroup arrival, not a memory-counter drain (`CDNA3 ISA §4.3–4.4 p.19-20`). No gfx12 `vscnt`/wait instructions. HIP twin uses equivalent staging barriers and compiler-generated waits with the same dataflow.
- No `v_permlane16/x16` in gfx942 twins; width32 software subgroup work uses ds_bpermute and subgroup masks. F32→FP8 conversion partial-destination hazards need an intervening instruction/NOP between writes to the same packed register (`CDNA3 ISA §7.2 p.51`). **ISA correction:** packed conversion ignores instruction `clamp`; saturation overflow is controlled by `FP16_OVFL`, and SH_MEM_CONFIG bit8 is required (`CDNA3 ISA §7.2 p.50-52`). The bounded≤112 producer does not rely on overflow saturation.


#### Decode shared design keys


These design IDs used in every kernel row specify both PM mapping and numerical fold; **an existing portable HIP source still needs a new PM gfx942 code object**.

- **V32 (canonical decode VALU):** wave64 with two independent logical width32 groups, index `lane=l&31`, `group=l>>5`; one output channel/group (or the incumbent's fixed channel bundle). Explicit `ds_bpermute_b32` addresses stay within the half; retain incumbent eight-term FMA DAG and ascending group/K order. No MFMA, no FP8, no LDS unless row explicitly specifies staging. Retain half-wave 32-thread HIP launches when portable; a full64 twin can pair two rows only with masked tails and the same DAG. PM decode DAG is not arbitrary associative math: MQ4 uses `t=fma(sc,q,zp)`, then a pinned pair-product order; single-row element1-first, dual-row row0 element0-first / row1 element1-first (`crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/pm_decode/mod.rs:9-26`). A HIP twin must use explicit `fmaf` and contraction-off if exactness is required, not trust equal expressions.
- **VB (block VALU):** retain exact row-local elementwise/reduction/SFU sequence; fixed block256 (4 wave64), width32 shuffles where source uses logical groups, and the same LDS tree/intermediate storage. FNUZ fold **none** unless a KV codec row says otherwise. Preserve BF16 RNE storage boundaries. PM SFU parity is `[UNVERIFIED: confirm on MI300X]`.
- **KV-P (FP8 producer):** preserve OCP row layout `[KVheads*D codes][KVheads F16 scales]`. Compute original per-head `s16`, including zero/underflow/upward-ulp rules; encode `cvt_pk_fp8_f32((x/s16)*0.5)` on gfx942. Normalize OCP `0x80` signed-zero to `0x00` rather than let it become FNUZ NaN. QSA exact rules are `amax/448`, zero→1, smallest F16 subnormal floor and one-ulp bump (`kernels/src/tensor_ops.hip:3122-3150`). This is a codec twin, **not FP8 activation quantization**.
- **KV-D (FP8 consumer):** packed `v_cvt_pk_f32_fp8` produces FNUZ values; multiply by **`2*s16` before dot/softmax/PV** to recover OCP values. Do not multiply final attention output by2: nonlinear softmax requires corrected K before scores. Q stays F32, decoded V is scaled once. Q8 sibling uses signed-i8×F32-scale, no FNUZ fold. OCP→FNUZ factor2 follows the bias8/max240/0x80-NaN format (`CDNA3 ISA §7.2 p.50-52`); the external design theorem is `subq4/review/GFX942-FP8-FN-PLAN.md:90-101,308-309` (outside source tree). Codes differ only at signed zero; sign-of-zero bit identity is not claimed.
- **MF16 alternative (explicitly different numerical arm):** for repeated/few-row dense projection, pad activation rows to16; choose A=weights[output,K], B=activation[K,token], `v_mfma_f32_16x16x32_fp8_fp8`: lane l owns A[l%16,8*(l/16)+0..7], B[8*(l/16)+0..7,l%16], C/D item r = D[4*(l/16)+r,l%16], four accum VGPRs (`CDNA3 ISA §7.1.4 p.46-48`). If both operands are folded FP8, apply **4*a*s** to each scale block's product before combining blocks; producer uses `(x/a)*0.5`. For weight-only BF16 projection use `v_mfma_f32_16x16x16_bf16` with four BF16 inputs/lane; decode FP8 weights with `2s` then BF16 RNE, **not** FP8 activations. Neither MFMA arm is a byte-identical substitute for canonical V32; preserve same-named lever and require separate KLD/identity receipt. No int4 MFMA exists: MQ4/MQ6 retain VALU or explicitly unpack to i8/BF16, never reinterpret nibble bytes as FP8.
- PM wave64 metadata requires 64-bit EXEC/VCC, architecture VGPR/AGPR allocation (up to256 each), and LDS≤64KiB (`CDNA3 ISA §3.1 p.8-9; §3.6.4 p.12-13; §11.1 p.84`). Bank layout is32×4B; stage row-major dwords with padded strides if a transpose conflicts. Replace gfx10+ permlane/WMMA encodings with DPP/ds_swizzle/ds_bpermute/readlane (`CDNA3 ISA §13.3.9 p.544-545; §11.2 p.85-87`). `vmcnt` counts loads **and stores**, including mixed issue order; `lgkmcnt` is4 bits; no gfx12 vscnt encoding. Explicitly wait before LDS readers/barriers; barrier alone is not memory completion (`CDNA3 ISA §4.3-4.4 p.19-20`). Unknown timing/MFMA internal accumulation order remains `[UNVERIFIED: confirm on MI300X]`.


#### FP8 KV design keys


**Amax256:** retain block256, four physical wave64s. Lowest-risk exact logical mapping preserves eight width32 groups: `ds_bpermute_b32` byte address `4*((lane&~31)|((lane&31)^offset))`, offsets16..1, `v_max_f32`; lanes0/32 publish slots `tid>>5`. Eight contiguous 4B LDS slots (32B), lgkm wait, barrier, scan8, barrier before K→V reuse. This reproduces source indexing exactly; `>>5` is a **logical group**, not physical wave ID. Alternative optimized finite-input mapping: ds_swizzle XOR1,2,4,8,16,32 over64, lane0 of each physical wave publishes `amax[tid>>6]` (4 slots/16B), scan4. DPP suffix-reduction row_shr1,2,4,8 plus row_bcast15 then row_bcast31 can publish a full-wave maximum from lane63; do not treat bcast31 as a full64 broadcast. Full-wave broadcast via ds_bpermute/readlane+scalar broadcast. For finite fabs/max these reductions are order-independent; NaN/sign-mode handling needs explicit comparison to source. DPP controls: CDNA3 ISA §13.3.9 pp.544–545; LDS32 banks×4B/64KiB: §2.2.1 p.6. No permlane16/x16 on this target.

**Dense eight-values/thread encoder:** either preserve block32 (half-populated wave64, all references remain within active lanes) or use block64, four values/lane and XOR32..1. Prefer block32 for stored-byte reference twin; block64 is a separate scheduling choice. Explicit width32 avoids relying on default shuffle width. Packed CVT operands must be VGPRs; retain independent registers/write spacing.

**VALU attention:** preserve token ownership, serial dimension/order chains, online softmax/gate and logical width32 max slots; no MFMA necessary for these twins. LDScode/score arrays remain indexed by thread, not physical wave. All EXEC/VCC manipulation becomes B64. `vmcnt` tracks loads **and stores** in issue order; `lgkmcnt` for LDS; wait before consuming/publishing, barrier does not replace memory waits (CDNA3 ISA §3.3 p.9; §4.4 pp.19–20).

**Gathered F16 MFMA:** preserve the derived F16 K and block-transposed V byte layout and F16 Q/P rounding, replace WMMA with `v_mfma_f32_16x16x16_f16` wave64. For lane l: A row `l%16`, K items `4*(l/16)+0..3` packed into2 VGPRs; B corresponding K rows/column `l%16`; C/D item r=`D[4*(l/16)+r][l%16]`,4 accumulators. Keep128-selected-token tile; four wave64s each process two16-token subtiles to cover original eight16-token tiles. LDS Q `[dc][head][16]`, P `[subtile][head][16]`, token indices, max/sum slots reallocated by **subtile**, not physical wave. ≤64KiB including dynamic token list. FNUZ→F32 `(2s)` then F16 RNE conversion is outside MFMA, so no additional ×4 here. MFMA layout: CDNA3 ISA §7.1.4 pp.46–48. Output is not guaranteed WMMA-bit-identical.

**Dense FP8 stage-b MFMA:** `16x16x32_fp8_fp8`, wave64 A/B8 bytes/lane in VGPR pair; D4 floats with mapping above, Q/K codes unchanged except zero handling. QK epilogue ×4*sq*sk (both codes folded), retaining scale placement per token; FP8-P/PV separately account for folded P and folded V, ×4*sp*sv. If P producer actually emits unfurled FNUZ rather than OCP-compatible half-valued codes, factor changes: choose the half-valued-byte contract consistently. F16 Q0 uses the preceding F16 MFMA design. Matrix accumulation order differs from WMMA and is not documented internally: `[UNVERIFIED: confirm error/KLD on MI300X]`.

#### Launch-order context / conditional chains



**Dense order:** DeltaNet1→2→3→4→(GDNscan,out of R1)→5→6→7→8→9→10; FA11→12→(attention,out of R1)→13→14→15 (`prefill.rs:14450-14501,14503-14540`). Rows6/8/10/12/14 each include a fresh #2repack before their F2 consumer; repack+consumer is one stream transaction (`gemm.rs:10332-10335`). Row15 expands R1.7–10 in order at FA sites `prefill.rs:10830,11131,11349,11384`. Rows16–20 are conditional replacement/off-route arms, not extra launches on the default512-row F2 sequence.

#### Launch-order context / conditional chains


`P`=provisioned grouped-row capacity; `S`=source-proved symmetric IU4 predicate; `B`=non-sym path2; `T55`=proposed gfx942 qt55 generator. Numeric alternatives are exclusive unless an explicit multi-kernel chain is named.


**Existing symmetric order:** 0/prelude→1a or1b→2S.1→2S.2→2S.3→3S.1→3S.2 or3S.2h→5S.1→5S.2 or5S.2h→6S.1→6S.2 or6S.2z. **Non-sym grouped order:** 0/prelude→router→2B→(3B.1→3B.2, or3B.3/3B.4)→4B or4B.alt→5B→6B; ≤8rows may instead take the indexed-down path cited above. Prelude router/shared BF16 projection GEMMs are distinct from expert census rows (`qt44_qt53_prefill.rs:308-320,336-360,374-378`). Indexed and generic atomic paths are scope exclusions from T55, not fabricated additional kernel symbols.

**Proposed T55 order:** natural prelude/router(no0rotation)→2S.1→2S.2→2S.3→G1 andG0→G→4T55→D0→D→6B. G1 adapter is once for both GEMMs and may precede G0 (independent inputs); G0/G1 both must precede G. The reused stable chain has independent T55 admission, not sym-IU4 selection. Generator arm never admits teacher/capture or numeric fallback.

PM source-tree bundles are SHA-pinned `kernels/qwen4_moe_iu4_sym_pm_gfx1201.hxaco`, SHA256 `44177c21de25bec8502f63f318e682dc4fa1948420723c75dec14fa63cfed723`, embedded at `crates/rdna-compute/src/kernels.rs:4220-4232`. The PM symbol generator emits anchor/NT4/NT8 names (`qwen4_moe_sym.rs:102-129`). A gfx942 twin needs new wave64 typed emission/contracts/binary pin; changing the architecture string or executing the gfx1201 bundle is not a port.

#### Launch-order context / conditional chains


These chains index every detailed table below; alternatives replace parent rows rather than accumulating unconditional work.

- **Qwen35 input/layer/tail:** embedding `0` (one artifact alternative) → per-layer common norm/basis `DN.1/FA.1` (or split1→split2) → **DN** `DN.2→3→4→5(if not compact)→6(one state arm)→7→8(if basis not supplied)→9`, or **FA** `FA.2→3→4(K writer)→5(V writer)→6/7(reader pair)` or scalar6 → `FA.8→9(if needed)→10` → `FFN.1→2→3→4`; final `tail.1→2→3` then caller sampling. Unfused conv uses `DN.4.off1→off2`; unfused FA prep uses `FA.3.off1→off2→off3→off4`. Layer sequence: `crates/hipfire-arch-qwen35/src/qwen35/forward.rs:6219-6252,6344-7248,7797-7885`.
- **Qwen4 input:** optional previous-logit argmax `0` → artifact embedding `1/1.mq` → streams init `2`; previous-token argmax is not a current-logit sampler (`crates/hipfire-arch-qwen4/src/gpu_forward.rs:6028-6074,6116-6126,6158-6173`).
- **Qwen4 per layer:** optional `PLE.1→2→3→4→5→6→7` → `HRA.1→2→3→4` → **GDN** `G.1(qkv,a,b,z)→2→3(one recurrence arm; generic adds gate)→4`, or **QSA** `Q.1(indexer,Q-gate,K,V)→2(one codec prologue)→3→4.score→5.select` (or fused/serial/exact selector) → `Q.6→7→8(if no selector mirror)` → `HWA.1→2→3` → `HRM.1→2→3→4` → MoE → `HWM.1→2→3`. Omit norms/gates/rotations only when producer-readiness/basis predicates say so. Source order: `crates/hipfire-arch-qwen4/src/gpu_forward.rs:5653,5747,5787,5837,5897,5913,5956-5960,5981`.
- **Qwen4 M1 MoE:** `MoE.0` clear → basis rotation `MoE.0.basis` before GateSide/Route if needed → `MoE.1` router/selector/shared GU (Q8 GU instead adds gate→up) → `MoE.2` route → `MoE.4` indexed GU → `MoE.5` BF16 activation/rotate (or F32a→F32b) → `MoE.6` expanded down → optional deferred round-trip → `MoE.7` expert-ID combine → unfused shared activation `MoE.8` if not produced earlier → shared-down `MoE.9` or other projection→scaled-add. No invented MoE.3. Source: `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/moe_program.rs:1370-1378`; `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/mod.rs:2971-3004,3186-3194,3284-3345,3530,3756-3794,3876-3897,4396-4407`.
- **Actual M2…8 MoE:** `B.0→B.1→B.1.activation(if not shared-in-unscatter)→B.2`; **path2** `B.3.path2→B.4.path2→B.5.path2`, or **path1** `B.4.path1→B.5.path1`; both use indexed `B.6→B.7→B.8.proj→B.8.add`. Small-row down recursively switches to path1; no grouped down GEMM. Source: `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/moe_program.rs:1519-1530,1580-1582`; `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/qt44_qt53_prefill.rs:1049-1060,1300-1352,1396-1398,1465-1466,1513-1522,1574-1575,1605-1613`.
- **QSA alternatives:** prologue fallback `off1→off2` (or F32a→F32b) → `off3(Full only)→off4→off5`; selection reuse `reuse1→reuse2→P.out`; actual M2…8 may use hg4, not hg12. PLE BF16 tail replaces ordinary gate/norm/conv/add with `bf16.1→2→3`; persistent GDN replaces singleton step only for actual rows>1. Exact local predicates/citations remain in the conditional table.
- **Qwen4 final HC/head:** `tail.1→2→3→4→5→6(optional current top1)`; generic down inserts HC activation. Source: `crates/hipfire-arch-qwen4/src/gpu_forward.rs:6575,6592,6608`; `crates/hipfire-arch-qwen4/src/program.rs:376-404,583-619`.
- **P expansion:** rotate once per shared MQ-basis/K unless already supplied, then project matrices in parent order. M2…8 uses independent token accumulators, not a reduction across tokens. C8 gfx1201 AR is all-per-lane, not automatically M8 (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/layer_ops.rs:316-379`; `crates/hipfire-arch-qwen4/src/gpu_forward_lanes.rs:211-265`).
- **Qwen35 Q8 sibling:** K→V writers or one pair writer; then scalar attention or tile→reduce. Replaces native-FP8 codec rows, never legitimizes mixed authored native+V-Q8 (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/attention.rs:530-576,1305-1306`).
- **Sampler:** optional penalty→fast partial→fast finalize; tie sentinel reruns exact partial→exact finalize with original RNG/adjusted logits. Exact path can be selected initially; single-block sampler/row sampler/argmax are caller alternatives. Detailed sampling table retains block/LDS budgets and all launch sites.




Main lowered binding is `crates/hipfire-arch-qwen35/src/qwen35/forward.rs:6344-7248`; final norm/head is `:7797-7885`. `DN.*` and `FA.*` are mutually exclusive layer branches. `P.*` is the producer/projection family expanded later; the row explicitly states where it appears. Existing HIP paths mean a source twin candidate, not an accepted PM binary.


#### Qwen4 Flash-Next per-token kernel table

Per-layer ordering below is the single-row F32-HC-stream route. `G.*` and `Q.*` are mutually exclusive GDN/QSA branches. `HRA/HWA/HRM/HWM` mean attention-read/write and MLP-read/write. Stateful work is performed separately per lane; the same source routine is not made batch-invariant by renaming it. Projection rows `P.*` expand through the following projection table at each cited call, in its declared matrix order.


#### Projection / few-row lane expansion table

Insert P.rotate immediately before the first MQ-basis projection unless the preceding nonlinear producer already supplied that basis. A shared-input group rotates once and launches its members in the order specified by the parent table (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/pipeline/layer_ops.rs:316-379`). For M2…8, weight rows are reused but each token retains a separate accumulator. This is **not** an assumption that gfx1201 has gfx1151's lane G0 sharing receipt.


#### Conditional QSA / HC / few-row state alternatives

These replace parent rows, in the listed local order. They are not extra unconditional work for the fixed Flash-Next M1 geometry.


#### Named PM bundles: exact coverage and excluded decode accelerations

- `HIPFIRE_PM_DECODE` accepted runtime table has **five** modules: QKVZA, QKV, gate/up, multirow-r2 plain GEMV, residual GEMV (`crates/rdna-compute/src/pm_decode_twins.rs:87-128`). Whole source/image/ELF SHA admission fails closed (`:14-27,158-170`); embedded files are `kernels/pm-decode/gfx1201/<module>.hxaco` (`:67-80`). Neither a source hash nor a gfx1201 ELF is gfx942 acceptance.
- Builder author inventory has **eight**, adding residual-xbatch, plain-xbatch and rows-GEMM (`crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/pm_decode/mod.rs:50-58`). Wave32/zero-spill/zero-LDS contract is explicit (`:89-98`). The three extras do not replace incumbent exports through the five-entry PM_DECODE table.
- Qwen4 `qwen4_lane_rows_pm_gfx1151.hxaco` exports four named lane rows, pin `11d0c2c7e423ef211e8c32202d20deea3d317b7f2a7ae538b069b56978c60a22` (`crates/rdna-compute/src/kernels.rs:4304-4320`). Its auto scope is gfx1151 only and no record/capture (`crates/rdna-compute/src/lane_rows_pm.rs:143-148`).


#### Actual M2…8 MoE grouped / indexed alternatives

Only actual few-row forwards use these rows; gfx1201 C8 AR with all-per-lane policy still uses the M1 table. QT44/QT53 path2 can be disabled for narrow host-mapped verify by generic resolution (`crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/moe.rs:1685-1737`); then path1 is fully indexed. The small-row override for offloaded matrices requires routed-down MQ4G256/V2, so does not admit QT53 (`:1680-1684`). Symmetric IU4 and grouped F16 WMMA remain excluded below512 rows.


#### Qwen35 Q8 sibling, not a mixed native-FP8 pair

For explicitly split q8/q8 mode the native-FP8 table is replaced locally by the following codec family. This does not make `--kv-mode fp8 --kv-v q8` admissible.


#### Sampling / greedy tail kernel table

Sampling is caller-dependent, not an unconditional part of `forward_gpu`: Qwen35 singleton serve can use CPU sampling (`crates/hipfire-arch-qwen35/src/serve_engine.rs:2179-2198`), while device/batch callers use the GPU sampler (`crates/hipfire-runtime/src/sampler.rs:170`; `crates/hipfire-runtime/src/batch_sampler.rs:190-335`). Blocked-token `-INF` writes and history upload are stream memory operations. One request's optional penalty→partial→finalize sequence remains in request order; the tie rerun uses the original RNG and already-adjusted logits.


Qwen4's generic VMM graph relocation explicitly refuses captured hipGraph resources (“Qwen4 never captures graphs”) at `crates/rdna-compute/src/graph.rs:616-625`; retained replay is a distinct transport. Do not interpret generic graph flags as proof that Qwen4 has a hipGraph capture route.

#### Launch-order context / conditional chains
#### QSA



PM bundle binding is `crates/rdna-compute/src/kernels.rs:4243-4244` (`kernels/qsa_gather_pm_gfx1201.hxaco`); score/select bundle `:4258-4260`, BF16 score `:4381-4385`. Do not reuse gfx1201 hxaco on gfx942; new arch-specific bundle/contracts/pins required. Only PM gather **converter** reads FP8 cache, gather attend reads derived F16; score/topk read index state, not FP8 KV.


#### Dense native





## 4. FNUZ fold rules (one numerical contract)

CDNA3 FP8 is E4M3 FNUZ: bias 8, max 240, subnormal quantum 2^-10, `0x80` NaN and only unsigned zero (CDNA3 ISA §7.2 p.50–51; `/home/kaden/qcal/release-0.4.2/pm-cdna3-ref/cdna3-isa.pages.txt:3123-3209`). OCP E4M3FN has bias 7, max 448 and signed zero. The exact finite-code reinterpretation, including subnormals, is `FNUZ(b)=OCP(b)/2` **after** remapping OCP `0x80` to `0x00`; refuse OCP NaNs `0x7f/0xff` rather than treating their FNUZ finite interpretations as valid (`/home/kaden/qcal/release-0.4.2/subq4/review/GFX942-FP8-FN-PLAN.md:90-101`, §3.2).

* **Activation producer:** preserve row scale `a`; convert `(x/a)*0.5` with FNUZ conversion. This reproduces finite OCP codes in the declared range, except canonical +0 replaces negative zero. Preserve the original producer arithmetic (multiplication versus IEEE division is not interchangeable). Half-sum sidecars remain in the OCP domain, not half-valued FNUZ sums (`crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/cdna/fp8_act_producer.rs:12-24`; PLAN §13.2 `:314-317`).
* **Weight/KV decode:** stored OCP nonzero finite bytes read as half values; apply `2*s` in **F32**, not a doubled F16 cache scale. Remap artifact weight `0x80` in operand registers; the new KV producer never emits it. Signed-zero byte identity is not promised (`crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/cdna/fp8_moe_gate_up.rs:60-67`; `crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/cdna/qsa_fp8_kv.rs:33-57`; PLAN §13.1 `:307-312`).
* **FNUZ × FNUZ MFMA:** both operands halve, hence restore `4*a*s_w` in the epilogue. For qt55, accumulate four K32 MFMAs per K128 block, scale-add blocks ascending, no split-K or floating atomics; `s_inv` is a multiplier. Byte equality is not WMMA/MFMA output equality, and this generator arm is not the BF16-operand teacher/capture route (`crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/cdna/fp8_moe_gate_up.rs:50-58`; PLAN §13.2 `:314-317`).
* **KV scale correction:** source performs a **single** capped F16 one-ulp increment (`if`), not the plan's "until" loop. Its finite-domain ceiling remains relevant; do not silently implement repeated bumps (`crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/cdna/qsa_fp8_kv.rs:25-42,59-64`; PLAN §13.1 `:308`). Conversion overflow/denorm and rounding details require hardware confirmation, not a claim of measured parity (CDNA3 ISA §7.2 p.50–51).

### Dense MQ4 consumer compensation (D1b–d)

- **(b) Weights.** The repack LUT `q→E4M3(q−8)` (`mq4v2_fp8_fragment_repack.gfx12.hip:85-99`; symfold table `gemm_gate_up_mq4g256v2_wmma_fp8.gfx12.hip:111-127`) contains no 0x80 (q=8 → 0x00; zero-scale halves → 0x00, repack `:147-158`). No remap is needed. FNUZ value = (q−8)/2.
- **(c) Product.** FNUZ×FNUZ = (x/a)(q−8)/4 → C₉₄₂ = C₁₂₀₁/4 exactly, for the same summation order. Fold ×4 into an exact power-of-two multiply:
  - F2: `D' = 4·D` at D staging. The epilogue is `RN(D·RN(Ew·C))` (`epilogue.rs:114-120`), and RN(4D·RN(Ew·C/4)) = RN(D·RN(Ew·C)).
  - v2/bt: `s_h' = 4·s_h` at LDS staging, so `fma(4s_h, P/4, T) = fma(s_h, P, T)`.
  - Rw ratios are unchanged.
- **(d) Half sums.** The in-register path decodes with `__builtin_amdgcn_cvt_pk_f32_fp8` (`mq4v2_fp8_producer_pack.hip:206-209`), which is FNUZ on gfx942 and gives value/2. The twin multiplies `lane_parts` by 2 (exact) or uses the software OCP decoder `fp8stream_e4m3_to_f32` (`:39-54`) on the identical byte. The from-F32 path already uses the software decoder (`:144-147`) and is unchanged.

These are actual module-doc designs, not proof of runtime registration or MI300X execution. Each has a HIP twin and an independent-reference test file; no checks were run for this documentation task.

| `crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/cdna/fp8_act_producer.rs:5-24,86-92` | **Plain F32 pack**, not RMS/FWHT/AWQ fused producer. One wave64/workgroup per row, K positive/divisible256; codes `[N,K]`, sums `[N,K/256,2]`, F32 scales `[N]`; mode1 power-of-two scale including shift -1..3, other modes unit scale. Reproduces source RN reciprocal-based amax exponent/clamps; `ldexp(x,-e-1)` FNUZ encode; OCP-decode emitted bytes for original half-sum/XOR tree. No MFMA. | R1 standalone `pack_f32_to_fp8_mq4v2_gfx12` row; numerical basis for R2 proposed natural-basis qt55 gate/up and down producers, not a completed fused producer family. | `kernels/src/pm_cdna/fp8_act_producer.gfx942.hip`; `crates/peacemaker-isa/tests/cdna_fp8_act_producer.rs` |
| `crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/cdna/fp8_moe_gate_up.rs:5-105` | qt55 native-basis grouped gate/up **and down** (`x_row_div=10/1`); block256/four wave64, grid `[R/64,n_tiles,1]`. Transposed `16x16x32_fp8_fp8`: A weights/B activations; lane mapping `A[l%16,8*(l/16)+i]`, `B[8*(l/16)+i,l%16]`, D four rows per lane. No atomics or split-K. | R2 proposed T55 G/D grouped GEMMs; does **not** reuse QT44/QT53 IU4 code objects or imply a gfx1201 FP8 MoE arm. | `kernels/src/pm_cdna/fp8_moe_gate_up.gfx942.hip`; `crates/peacemaker-isa/tests/cdna_fp8_moe_gate_up.rs` |

No MFMA. | R4 E1/D1 QSA row-codec helpers; foundation for R3 QSA FP8 decode prologue and R4 append/read consumers, **not** complete route integration. | `kernels/src/pm_cdna/qsa_fp8_kv.gfx942.hip`; `crates/peacemaker-isa/tests/cdna_qsa_fp8_kv.rs` |

## 6. Ordered port plan and needs MI300X hardware confirmation

The order below is a **proposed dependency plan**, not an implementation-completion claim. All admissions stay fail-closed until corresponding twins and numerical receipts exist.

1. **Finish the three branch kernels and their CPU-reference/HIP contracts first:** plain FP8 producer → qt55 grouped gate/up (same tile down); independently QSA row encode/decode. Preserve their actual module-doc contracts from §5. These are the minimum numerical building blocks, not fused route replacements (`crates/peacemaker-isa/src/kernels/cdna/{fp8_act_producer,fp8_moe_gate_up,qsa_fp8_kv}.rs`, cited ranges in §5).
2. **Port portable control/metadata and ordinary decode first:** retained width32 VALU chains, stable scatter ranks→prefix→scatter and tile descriptors, unscatter/SiLU and fixed-order combine, norms/RoPE/index append/pool/selection; replace permlanex16-dependent fast-router/GDN/O4/O8 reductions with logical32 wave64 twins before enabling them. Do not reuse gfx1201 `.hxaco` or infer gfx942 eligibility from portable arithmetic (R2 census/summary; R3 design contracts and launch census; `kernels/src/gated_delta_net_q8_fast.hip:142`; `crates/rdna-compute/src/kernels.rs:8632-8635,8728-8731`).
3. **Integrate QSA FP8 state before its accelerated attention:** codec → append and fused prologue → scalar/parallel/hg4 readers → gathered F16 converter → wave64 gathered F16 MFMA. Retain one-time scale bump, IEEE division, F32 ×2 decode, BF16 raw/pooled index state and separate derived-workspace charge. Widen runtime admission, compute format checks, dispatch registration and new bundle/SHA bindings together; widening `QSA_HAS_FP8` alone is insufficient (`crates/hipfire-runtime/src/kv_mode.rs:548-575`; `tensor_ops.rs:2530-2593,2727-2747,4897-4905,5081-5140`; `crates/rdna-compute/src/kernels.rs:4243-4244,4258-4260,4381-4385`).
4. **Integrate qt55 native-FP8 generator MoE as a new arm:** natural-basis producer G → grouped gate/up → declared BF16 SiLU boundaries → natural-basis producer D → same grouped tile down → fixed-order combine. Preserve padded M1 through the same grouped family, no indexed different-order fallback, no atomic CDNA down_path0, no hidden rotated QT44 input; separately admit proposed generator flag and PM/HIP control. Weight-only BF16-MFMA teacher remains distinct (R2 proposed qt55 contract; `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/families/moe.rs:1740`; PLAN §13.2 `:314-317`).
5. **Mirror dense 27B prefill:** fused RMS/FWHT/AWQ and SwiGLU/HIN FP8 producers → portable fragment repack → F2 Row ratio-fold MFMA tiles/epilogues → smaller-row v2/bt variants. Retain Wf/Rw/Ew/half-sums domain and symfold compensation; resolve same family flags on gfx942 (IU4 already false). **GDN fused prep remains blocked by gfx942 GDN chunk scan**; use explicitly admitted plain producer/projection/portable recurrence paths, never pretend the scan is a simple arch gate. GDN scan requires its own wave64 MFMA recurrence/scan design and numerical evidence (`prefill.rs:14407-14412,6582-6617,7521-7581`; `gemm.rs:10293-10329,10827-10836`; R1 contracts D1–D3/kernel table and resolution sites).
6. **Dense KV integration/accelerations:** single/batched/paged/VMM/lane writers and scalar/flash readers first; GQA tile/reducer pair next; F16-Q0 FA2 before FP8 stage-b/packet/Q-resident v1/v2/A4 epilogues. Keep producer/Q/P fold boundaries explicit and preserve descriptors and masks. Only then widen native carrier/table/slots eligibility and frontend admission with exact model geometry; reconcile the legacy harness spelling explicitly. Charge real MI300X free/mapped/reserved memory, not R9700 constants (`crates/hipfire-arch-qwen35/src/carrier.rs:780-810`; `crates/hipfire-dispatch/src/tables/attention_table.rs:45-51,207-217`; `attention.rs:6160-6228,6575-6580,6831-7008`; `crates/rdna-compute/src/slot_pool.rs:115-116`; `page_pool.rs:378-379`; R4 arena/admission section).
7. **Route certification precedes default widening:** first-load and replay/graph PM-vs-HIP whole-buffer/state/EF identity, row-count/lane G0, then generator-only KLD for new MFMA/FP8-state arms. Do not promote the 27B gfx1201 pin, gfx1151 G0 or BF16 teacher result into a gfx942 receipt (R2 open questions; R3 required evidence; R4 final validation list). No GPU or builds were run for this document.

Explicitly wait before LDS readers/barriers; barrier alone is not memory completion (`CDNA3 ISA §4.3-4.4 p.19-20`).
